Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in space theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 211 and the invariant I(A,B) = 229 mod 2.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the space decomposition. By minimality, the element a^211 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 229-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
